- **verifier**: Verification specialist that validates task outputs against Given/When/Then specs using multi-layer analysis (syntax, tests, behavior, edge cases, code quality). ([MaxwellCalkin/autonomous-autonomy](https://github.com/MaxwellCalkin/autonomous-autonomy/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent check of swarm work: a ticket's READY head (phase=read), its merged commit (phase=merged, alone calling swarm.py pass), an epic's agent gates (phase=gates). Use when swarm-run hands over a head, a merged PR or gates due. Not a PR review; never for the change's author. ([MdaaaaO/ai-baton](https://github.com/MdaaaaO/ai-baton/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the gates and approves or rejects a merge. Use after implementation to run tests, evals, and security checks and give a pass/fail verdict. ([mdrewt/monster-realm](https://github.com/mdrewt/monster-realm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))

- **verifier**: Runs the `verify` stage: dispatches four review agents in parallel - the three `reviewer` lenses plus the security one - reconciles what they report, runs this repo's test command, and fixes only Blocker/Major, test-first. ([millerlai/claude-all-in-one](https://github.com/millerlai/claude-all-in-one/blob/HEAD/plugins/cai/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the repository's documented checks and tests and reports every result verbatim, separating pass, fail, blocked, and not-installed. Read-only: it never edits. - **verifier**: You check a finished agent setup the way a brand-new session would meet it. You were deliberately not given the plan or the reasoning behind it: judge only what is on disk. - **verifier**: Adversarial verification in a fresh context - tries to REFUTE a claim, diff, or finding and demands evidence. Use as /research's claim gate, for verifying a single claim, and before declaring work done. ([MohammedMoataz/ai-coach](https://github.com/MohammedMoataz/ai-coach/blob/HEAD/plugins/atlas-coach/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Judges whether work actually passes, using command output as evidence. Never edits code. Use whenever a loop cycle needs a verdict. ([MohdAljahdali/mjloop](https://github.com/MohdAljahdali/mjloop/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs a phase's acceptance criteria as tests and gates the merge via goal-backward analysis. Spawn at the verification step; writes verification reports, never mutates source or tests. ([mrflxxxme/oriion](https://github.com/mrflxxxme/oriion/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: CGEL semantic verifier — read-only review of a described change against the project's semantic rules (docs/standards). Returns findings as JSON only. Invoke when the sealed contract requires semantic verification, after deterministic checks pass and before proposing PASS. The main agent passes the changed file list and the rule ids in the prompt. ([mthanhlm/cgel-plugin](https://github.com/mthanhlm/cgel-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Use after any code change, before reporting completion. Runs the full verification suite and reports the real command output. Never modifies code. Dispatch this instead of self-reporting that checks passed. ([mwathiben/hush-private-bookmarks](https://github.com/mwathiben/hush-private-bookmarks/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Adversarially verifies that a task is actually done. Use proactively before declaring any task complete. ([nada-r/agent-template](https://github.com/nada-r/agent-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the verification ladder and reads ALL logs so the main context never has to. Use PROACTIVELY after every feature and before any "done" claim. Returns PASS/FAIL verdicts only. ([nahiin/The-Phone](https://github.com/nahiin/The-Phone/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Puerta POST-implementacion (read-only) de Nexus, goal-backward. Deriva condiciones de exito desde el objetivo y verifica que el CODIGO REAL las cumple — caza stubs, TODO/FIXME, except pass, print() de debug; comprueba el wiring real de src/nexus/; corre pytest/ruff/mypy; revisa docs/memoria. Emite VERIFIED / ISSUES_FOUND / INCOMPLETE. Usar cuando el PM completa todas las tareas de un plan y necesita verificar antes de reportar "completado". ([Nambu89/Nexus](https://github.com/Nambu89/Nexus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Puerta POST-implementacion (read-only) de la plataforma world-intelligence, goal-backward. Deriva condiciones de exito desde el objetivo y verifica que el CODIGO REAL las cumple — caza stubs, TODO/FIXME, catch vacio, console.log; comprueba el wiring real del monorepo; corre build/tests/typecheck; revisa docs/memoria. Emite VERIFIED / ISSUES_FOUND / INCOMPLETE. Usar cuando el PM completa todas las tareas de un plan y necesita verificar antes de reportar "completado". ([Nambu89/world_wide_project](https://github.com/Nambu89/world_wide_project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independently runs the changed system and proves acceptance criteria hold, with pasted command output as evidence. - **verifier**: You verify a prepared modelo revision against the registry's expectations and the operator safety contract. ([nevenincs/neve-marketplace](https://github.com/nevenincs/neve-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/aeat/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Đối chiếu từng CLAIM trong một plan/answer với codebase THẬT, trả về GROUNDED/UNVERIFIABLE/FABRICATED kèm `file:line`. Dùng sau `architect`, trước khi giao `builder`. KHÔNG dùng để phản biện logic (đó là `critic`), KHÔNG dùng để sửa code. ([nhattamgithub1999/agent-kit](https://github.com/nhattamgithub1999/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent check that a completion claim is true — reads the diff and runs the fitting gates itself instead of trusting the implementer's summary. - **verifier**: Independently verifies a stage by inspecting its diff against the contract and by running it. Writes command output to a log on disk. Never fixes. Emits VERDICT PASS or FAIL. - **verifier**: Fast deterministic checks — formatter, linter, type checker, log.json shape. Use for cheap pass/fail validation. Not a substitute for adversarial review. ([nym/caskagent](https://github.com/nym/caskagent/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Adversarial check of claimed-done work. Re-runs verification, hunts gaps between claim and evidence, actively tries to break the change. Verdict CONFIRMED or REFUTED with proof. ([odjacobs/claude-skills](https://github.com/odjacobs/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Use this agent AFTER implementation to verify acceptance criteria, contracts, security, and production readiness. - **verifier**: 구현이 끝나면 자동으로 사용한다(proactively). 특히 고위험 작업(돈·인증·삭제·배포)의 검증엔 필수. 구현과 분리된 신선한 컨텍스트에서 결과물을 PRD/PLAN 대비 검증하고, 수정 없이 판정과 근거만 보고한다. ([only-victory/agent-project-template](https://github.com/only-victory/agent-project-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs once per sitting close as a separately-dispatched reader — never the orchestrator self-certifying its own beat log. Performs ADR-001's five structural checks (append-only integrity, ledger coverage, beat-numbering parity, session-log schema, cross-file fact consistency), patches the verifier-owned derived-view fact fields from canon deltas, writes its terse report into the sitting's Close block, and issues one commit per sitting close where a git repository exists (git-optional; never a dependency). - **verifier**: Proves work actually behaves correctly by running it, not just checking that artifacts exist. Use immediately after any non-trivial task is claimed complete, before commit, before marking task done, and whenever the user asks to "verify that X works", "verify the function/file …", or to run tests and report the truth — delegate to this agent instead of running the tests in the parent session. Distinct from a code reviewer/critic — this agent runs the code and reports observed behavior, not opinions. ([sethdford/claude-agent-os](https://github.com/sethdford/claude-agent-os/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent verification specialist. Use to confirm a change actually works before it is declared done — runs the project's tests/lint/build, inspects the diff adversarially, and returns a PASS/FAIL verdict with evidence. Deliberately blind to the maker's reasoning and instructed to find reasons to reject. Invoke as the verify step of the compound loop; never let an implementer grade its own work. ([sethdford/claude-toolkit](https://github.com/sethdford/claude-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/compound-loop/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: MUST BE USED at the end of every implementation task BEFORE marking done. Runs tests, runs the app, exercises acceptance criteria, checks observability. Boris Cherny: "give the agent a way to verify and it improves the result." NEVER skip - this is what produces "first-try" quality. ([shakhovskiya-create/shakhoff-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/shakhovskiya-create/shakhoff-claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/solo-sdlc/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the Portfolio Forge verification harness against a generated site and reports precisely what failed and why. Use during phase 4, and after every fix attempt. ([sheikharfaz/portfolio-forge](https://github.com/sheikharfaz/portfolio-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Claim-by-claim audit of a Parallax draft issue. Reads the draft MDX and the research dossier, verifies every factual claim traces to a sourced dossier entry, checks for brand voice compliance, and writes a verification report. Use this agent after /pipeline-draft has written a draft and the editor has done a first read. This is the brand-protection step before publish. ([shikharsumantech14/parallax](https://github.com/shikharsumantech14/parallax/blob/HEAD/scripts/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent adversarial verifier for high-stakes review. Attempts to refute delegated work and returns separate spec-compliance and build-quality verdicts. Use a capability-matched model selected by the active harness. ([shubhamsingh-cell/fleetcraft](https://github.com/shubhamsingh-cell/fleetcraft/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Independently verifies production and/or test implementation against the original requirement, approved task contract, git diff and real execution. It does not trust implementer summaries and is the only agent allowed to issue PASS / FAIL / INCONCLUSIVE. ([si13n/forgetty-android](https://github.com/si13n/forgetty-android/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Executes reproductions to confirm or kill a candidate finding. Delegate a single candidate bug, quality concern, or improvement claim. The verifier writes a minimal repro/test or benchmark, runs it, and reports whether it actually reproduces, turning guesses into CONFIRMED or dropping them. It runs tests and benchmarks, and does not edit source under test. Run every repro/mutation/benchmark through `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/run-proof.mjs record -- &lt;cmd&gt;` so the run leaves a replayable receipt in `RUN_RECEIPTS.md`, because a claimed result with no receipt is narration, not proof. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/blob/HEAD/plugins/rigor/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Executes reproductions to confirm or kill a candidate finding. Delegate a single candidate bug, quality concern, or improvement claim. - **verifier-fast**: Gate 2 verifier for criteria-checkable output. Compares output against explicit done-criteria (IDs C1..Cn), pinned to a diff range. ([midego1/claude-orchestrate](https://github.com/midego1/claude-orchestrate/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-fast.md) · ★8)
- **verifier-integrity-hunter**: Reward-hack detection specialist — looks for test hardcoding, verifier bypass, require-tests evasion, and quiet requirement-narrowing. Takes the diff AND the run's trajectory (verdict LOG, TDD red→green history) as input, not just the final diff. - **verifier-medium**: Independently audits a diff against an approved PLAN.md — never against the implementer's own summary of what it did. Read-only. ([tammai/bigin-skills](https://github.com/tammai/bigin-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-medium.md))
- **verifier-product**: Tier 3 of a three-tier certification. Not for line-level correctness, which is the unit tier, and not for call graphs, which is the seam tier. Returns an eight-key tier report. Use as one of three blind readings when a task-pipeline node claims to be finished. ([joshzyj/open-scholar-skill](https://github.com/joshzyj/open-scholar-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verify-logic.md) · ★157)

- **verify-numerics**: A verification agent that performs Stage 1 numeric verification — comparing raw analysis outputs (CSVs, HTML tables, R/Python console output) against the formatted tables presented in the manuscript. Detects transcription errors, rounding mistakes, dropped rows/columns, and transformation errors introduced when moving from raw output to publication-ready tables. ([joshzyj/open-scholar-skill](https://github.com/joshzyj/open-scholar-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verify-numerics.md) · ★157)

- **verify-phase**: Codex-owned test-authoring and full-suite verification gate (stage 6): Codex designs and authors tests, the full suite runs locally, failures fix in a capped loop. - **verify-restatement**: Cross-paper claim verification. Spawn as a fresh subagent so the web-fetch + reading workload stays out of the main context. Returns a structured comparison report.
